pwct_reset_months = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_game_rule = { rule = pwct_cadence option = M3 } }
        set_variable = { global.pwct_months_left = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_game_rule = { rule = pwct_cadence option = M6 } }
        set_variable = { global.pwct_months_left = 6 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_game_rule = { rule = pwct_cadence option = M12 } }
        set_variable = { global.pwct_months_left = 12 }
    }
}
pwct_reset_weights = {
    set_variable = { pwct_w_1 = 24 }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_lv_1 > 2 } }
        set_variable = { pwct_w_1 = 0 }
    }
    set_variable = { pwct_w_2 = 24 }
    if = {
        limit = { OR = { check_variable = { pwct_lv_2 > 2 } check_variable = { pwct_c_army_attack_factor > 0.299 } } }
        set_variable = { pwct_w_2 = 0 }
    }
    set_variable = { pwct_w_3 = 24 }
    if = {
        limit = { OR = { check_variable = { pwct_lv_3 > 2 } check_variable = { pwct_c_army_defence_factor > 0.299 } } }

pwct_describe_offers = {

pwct_draw = {
    add_to_variable = { pwct_draw_count = 1 }
    set_variable = { pwct_slots = 3 }
    clr_country_flag = pwct_behind
    set_temp_variable = { pwct_states = num_owned_states }
    set_variable = { pwct_strong = pwct_states }
    multiply_variable = { pwct_strong = 3 }

        add_to_variable = { pwct_dry = 1 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { OR = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_1 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_offer_2 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_offer_3 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 > 0 } } }
        set_country_flag = pwct_pending
        set_country_flag = { flag = pwct_pending_window days = 30 }
        if = {
            limit = { is_ai = yes }
            pwct_ai_pick = yes
        }
        else = {
            country_event = { id = pwct.3 days = 30 }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { pwct_draw_count = 1 } }
                set_country_flag = pwct_panel_open
                clr_country_flag = pwct_collection_open
            }
            else = {
                country_event = { id = pwct.1 }
            }
        }
    }
}
pwct_draw_all = {
    set_variable = { global.pwct_omen = 0 }
    if = {
        limit = { has_game_rule = { rule = pwct_omens option = ON } }
        random_list = {
            10 = {
                set_variable = { global.pwct_omen = 1 }
            }
            10 = {
                set_variable = { global.pwct_omen = 2 }
            }
            10 = {
                set_variable = { global.pwct_omen = 3 }
            }
            10 = {
                set_variable = { global.pwct_omen = 4 }
            }
            10 = {
                set_variable = { global.pwct_omen = 5 }
            }
            10 = {
                set_variable = { global.pwct_omen = 6 }
            }
            10 = {
                set_variable = { global.pwct_omen = 7 }
            }
        }
    }
    every_country = {
        limit = { pwc_st_live_city_country = yes OR = { has_game_rule = { rule = pwct_ai_participation option = ALL } is_ai = no } }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = pwct_enabled } }
            pwct_init_country = yes
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = pwct_pending }
            pwct_auto_pick = yes
        }
        pwct_auto_upgrade = yes
        pwct_recompute = yes
        pwct_draw = yes
    }
}

pwct_expire_pending = {
    every_country = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = pwct_pending NOT = { has_country_flag = pwct_pending_window } }
        pwct_auto_pick = yes
    }
}
pwct_find_best_offer = {
    set_temp_variable = { pwct_best = 0 }
    set_temp_variable = { pwct_best_rar = -1 }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_1 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_1 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } check_variable = { pwct_offer_1_rar > pwct_best_rar } }
        set_temp_variable = { pwct_best = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { pwct_best_rar = pwct_offer_1_rar }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_2 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_2 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } check_variable = { pwct_offer_2_rar > pwct_best_rar } }
        set_temp_variable = { pwct_best = 2 }
        set_temp_variable = { pwct_best_rar = pwct_offer_2_rar }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_3 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_3 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } check_variable = { pwct_offer_3_rar > pwct_best_rar } }
        set_temp_variable = { pwct_best = 3 }
        set_temp_variable = { pwct_best_rar = pwct_offer_3_rar }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } check_variable = { pwct_offer_4_rar > pwct_best_rar } }
        set_temp_variable = { pwct_best = 4 }
        set_temp_variable = { pwct_best_rar = pwct_offer_4_rar }
    }
}
pwct_auto_pick = {
    pwct_find_best_offer = yes
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 0 } check_variable = { pwct_fallback > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_fallback = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } }
        set_variable = { pwct_offer_1 = pwct_fallback }
        pwct_describe_offers = yes
        set_temp_variable = { pwct_best = 1 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 1 } }
        pwct_pick_slot_1 = yes
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 2 } }
        pwct_pick_slot_2 = yes
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 3 } }
        pwct_pick_slot_3 = yes
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 4 } }
        pwct_pick_slot_4 = yes
    }
}

        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 26 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_26 = 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_8 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_10 > 0 } }
        multiply_variable = { pwct_ai_w_4 = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 26 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_26 = 0 } OR = { check_variable = { pwct_lv_8 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_10 > 0 } } }
        multiply_variable = { pwct_ai_w_4 = 1.5 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 9 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_9 = 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_25 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_27 > 0 } }
        multiply_variable = { pwct_ai_w_4 = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 9 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_9 = 0 } OR = { check_variable = { pwct_lv_25 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_27 > 0 } } }
        multiply_variable = { pwct_ai_w_4 = 1.5 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 25 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_25 = 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_9 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_27 > 0 } }
        multiply_variable = { pwct_ai_w_4 = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 25 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_25 = 0 } OR = { check_variable = { pwct_lv_9 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_27 > 0 } } }
        multiply_variable = { pwct_ai_w_4 = 1.5 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 27 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_27 = 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_9 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_25 > 0 } }
        multiply_variable = { pwct_ai_w_4 = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 27 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_27 = 0 } OR = { check_variable = { pwct_lv_9 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_lv_25 > 0 } } }
        multiply_variable = { pwct_ai_w_4 = 1.5 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } } }
        set_variable = { pwct_ai_w_4 = 0 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { OR = { check_variable = { pwct_ai_w_1 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_ai_w_2 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_ai_w_3 > 0 } check_variable = { pwct_ai_w_4 > 0 } } }
        random_list = {
            pwct_ai_w_1 = {
                pwct_pick_slot_1 = yes
            }
            pwct_ai_w_2 = {
                pwct_pick_slot_2 = yes
            }
            pwct_ai_w_3 = {
                pwct_pick_slot_3 = yes
            }
            pwct_ai_w_4 = {
                pwct_pick_slot_4 = yes
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = pwct_pending }
        pwct_auto_pick = yes
    }
}
pwct_reroll = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = pwct_pending }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { pwct_reroll_used = 0 } OR = { AND = { check_variable = { pwct_alt1_1 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_alt1_1 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } } AND = { check_variable = { pwct_alt1_2 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_alt1_2 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } } AND = { check_variable = { pwct_alt1_3 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_alt1_3 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } } AND = { check_variable = { pwct_alt1_4 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_alt1_4 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } } } has_political_power > 29 }
            pwct_find_best_offer = yes
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 1 } }
                set_variable = { pwct_fallback = pwct_offer_1 }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 2 } }
                set_variable = { pwct_fallback = pwct_offer_2 }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 3 } }
                set_variable = { pwct_fallback = pwct_offer_3 }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 4 } }
                set_variable = { pwct_fallback = pwct_offer_4 }
            }
            set_variable = { pwct_offer_1 = pwct_alt1_1 }
            set_variable = { pwct_offer_2 = pwct_alt1_2 }
            set_variable = { pwct_offer_3 = pwct_alt1_3 }
            set_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = pwct_alt1_4 }
            add_political_power = -30
            set_variable = { pwct_reroll_used = 1 }
            pwct_describe_offers = yes
            if = {
                limit = { OR = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_1_rar > 1 } check_variable = { pwct_offer_2_rar > 1 } check_variable = { pwct_offer_3_rar > 1 } check_variable = { pwct_offer_4_rar > 1 } } }
                set_variable = { pwct_dry = 0 }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { pwct_reroll_used = 1 } OR = { AND = { check_variable = { pwct_alt2_1 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_alt2_1 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } } AND = { check_variable = { pwct_alt2_2 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_alt2_2 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } } AND = { check_variable = { pwct_alt2_3 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_alt2_3 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } } AND = { check_variable = { pwct_alt2_4 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_alt2_4 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } } } has_political_power > 59 }
            pwct_find_best_offer = yes
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 1 } }
                set_variable = { pwct_fallback = pwct_offer_1 }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 2 } }
                set_variable = { pwct_fallback = pwct_offer_2 }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 3 } }
                set_variable = { pwct_fallback = pwct_offer_3 }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { check_variable = { pwct_best = 4 } }
                set_variable = { pwct_fallback = pwct_offer_4 }
            }
            set_variable = { pwct_offer_1 = pwct_alt2_1 }
            set_variable = { pwct_offer_2 = pwct_alt2_2 }
            set_variable = { pwct_offer_3 = pwct_alt2_3 }
            set_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = pwct_alt2_4 }
            add_political_power = -60
            set_variable = { pwct_reroll_used = 2 }
            pwct_describe_offers = yes
            if = {
                limit = { OR = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_1_rar > 1 } check_variable = { pwct_offer_2_rar > 1 } check_variable = { pwct_offer_3_rar > 1 } check_variable = { pwct_offer_4_rar > 1 } } }
                set_variable = { pwct_dry = 0 }
            }
        }
    }
}

pwct_close_offers = {
    clr_country_flag = pwct_pending
    clr_country_flag = pwct_pending_window
    set_variable = { pwct_fallback = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_offer_1 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_offer_2 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_offer_3 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_offer_4 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_alt1_1 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_alt1_2 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_alt1_3 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_alt1_4 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_alt2_1 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_alt2_2 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_alt2_3 = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_alt2_4 = 0 }
}
pwct_pick_slot_1 = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = pwct_pending check_variable = { pwct_offer_1 > 0 } OR = { NOT = { check_variable = { pwct_offer_1 = 36 } } capital_scope = { free_building_slots = { building = industrial_complex size > 0 include_locked = no } } } }
        set_temp_variable = { pwct_pick = pwct_offer_1 }
        pwct_apply_pick = yes
        set_variable = { pwct_last_pick = pwct_offer_1 }
        pwct_close_offers = yes
        pwct_describe_offers = yes
        pwct_recompute = yes
    }
}

pwct_recompute = {
    set_variable = { pwct_owned_count = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_combo_count = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_set_mil = 0 }
    set_variable = { pwct_set_pol = 0 }

pwct_play_14 = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_hand_14 > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { pwct_hand_14 = 1 }
        subtract_from_variable = { pwct_hand_total = 1 }
        add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = pwct_dm_strat_14 days = 90 }
    }
}
pwct_play_38 = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { pwct_hand_38 > 0 } FROM = { exists = yes } has_war_with = FROM }
        subtract_from_variable = { pwct_hand_38 = 1 }
        subtract_from_variable = { pwct_hand_total = 1 }
        if = {
            limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { pwct_hand_50 > 0 } } }
            FROM = {
                subtract_from_variable = { pwct_hand_50 = 1 }
                subtract_from_variable = { pwct_hand_total = 1 }
                set_variable = { pwct_hit_card = 38 }
                if = {
                    limit = { is_ai = no }
                    country_event = { id = pwct.4 }
                }
            }
            set_variable = { pwct_hit_card = 38 }
            if = {
                limit = { is_ai = no }
                country_event = { id = pwct.5 }
            }
        }
        else = {
            FROM = {
                add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = pwct_dm_strat_38 days = 60 }
                set_variable = { pwct_hit_card = 38 }
                if = {
                    limit = { is_ai = no }
                    country_event = { id = pwct.2 }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
